var WO_SERVICE_TYPES=["Residential","Commercial","Industrial"];

function diffWorkOrderChanges(before,after){
  let changes=[];
  let labelFor={caseStatus:"Status",serviceType:"Service Type",requestType:"Request Type",assetType:"Asset Type",quantitySize:"Asset Size",woNotes:"Work Order Notes",hotTicket:"Hot Ticket",dueDate:"Due Date",requestDate:"Requested Date",resolutionCode:"Resolution Code",contactName:"Contact"};
  Object.keys(labelFor).forEach(k=>{
    let a=before[k],b=after[k];
    if(String(a||"")!==String(b||"")){
      if(typeof a==="boolean"||typeof b==="boolean")changes.push(`Changed ${labelFor[k]} from ${a?"true":"false"} to ${b?"true":"false"}.`);
      else changes.push(`Changed ${labelFor[k]} from "${a||"(blank)"}" to "${b||"(blank)"}".`)
    }
  });
  return changes
}

function WoitStepBar({step,flow,onStepClick}){
  let steps=[
    {n:1,label:"Type"},
    {n:2,label:"Upload"},
    {n:3,label:"Mapping"},
    {n:4,label:"Preview"},
    {n:5,label:"Processing"},
    {n:6,label:flow==="close"?"Closed":"Imported"}
  ];
  return o.default.createElement("div",{className:"flex items-stretch px-5 pt-4 pb-3 shrink-0",style:{background:l.canvas}},
    steps.map((s,idx)=>{
      let state=s.n<step?"done":s.n===step?"active":"upcoming";
      let clickable=state==="done"&&s.n!==5&&!!onStepClick;
      let bg=state==="done"?l.mint:state==="active"?l.violet:l.surfaceHi2;
      let fg=state==="upcoming"?l.faint:"#fff";
      let clip=idx===0?"polygon(0 0,90% 0,100% 50%,90% 100%,0 100%)":idx===steps.length-1?"polygon(10% 0,100% 0,100% 100%,10% 100%,0% 50%)":"polygon(0 0,90% 0,100% 50%,90% 100%,0 100%,10% 50%)";
      return o.default.createElement("div",{key:s.n,onClick:clickable?()=>onStepClick(s.n):undefined,title:clickable?`Back to ${s.label}`:undefined,className:"flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold h-8"+(clickable?" hover:opacity-90":""),style:{flex:1,marginLeft:idx===0?0:-14,background:bg,color:fg,clipPath:clip,zIndex:steps.length-idx,transition:"background 0.25s ease",cursor:clickable?"pointer":"default"}},
        o.default.createElement("span",{style:{opacity:state==="upcoming"?0.85:1}},state==="done"?"\u2713":s.n),
        o.default.createElement("span",{className:"tracking-wide"},s.label)
      )
    })
  )
}

  let[resultsPage,setResultsPage]=(0,o.useState)(0);
  let[errorsPage,setErrorsPage]=(0,o.useState)(0);
  let fields=flow==="close"?WOIT_CLOSE_FIELDS:WOIT_IMPORT_FIELDS;
  let savedMapping=flow==="close"?WOIT_CLOSE_SAVED_MAPPING:WOIT_IMPORT_SAVED_MAPPING;
  let mappedFields=fields.filter(f=>mapping[f.key]);

  let stepScreenMap={1:"entry",2:"selectFile",3:"mapping",4:"preview"};

  let templates=((store&&store.woitTemplates)||[...WOIT_IMPORT_TEMPLATES_SEED,...WOIT_CLOSE_TEMPLATES_SEED]).filter(tp=>tp.flow===flow);
  let normalizeCol=s=>String(s||"").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,"");
  let applyTemplate=tplId=>{
    setSelectedTemplateId(tplId);
    let tpl=templates.find(tp=>tp.id===tplId);
    if(!tpl){setMapping({});setMatchMode("manual");return}
    let m={};fields.forEach(f=>{if(tpl.mapping[f.key])m[f.key]=tpl.mapping[f.key]});
    setMapping(m);setMatchMode("template")
  };
  let autoMatch=cols=>{
    let colsNorm=cols.map(normalizeCol);
    let matchedTpl=templates.find(tp=>{let vals=Object.values(tp.mapping).map(normalizeCol);return vals.length>0&&vals.every(v=>colsNorm.includes(v))});
    if(matchedTpl){applyTemplate(matchedTpl.id);return}
    let m={};
    fields.forEach(f=>{
      let fieldNorm=normalizeCol(f.key);
      let aliasList=(WOIT_FIELD_ALIASES[f.key]||[]).map(normalizeCol);
      let ix=colsNorm.findIndex(cn=>cn===fieldNorm||aliasList.includes(cn));
      if(ix>=0)m[f.key]=cols[ix]
    });
    setMapping(m);setSelectedTemplateId("");
    setMatchMode(Object.keys(m).length?"auto":"manual")
  };
  let handleFileChosen=f=>{
    if(!f)return;
    setFileName(f.name);setMapping({});setSelectedTemplateId("");setFileColumns([]);setMatchMode("manual");
    let reader=new FileReader();
    reader.onload=ev=>{
      try{
        let data=new Uint8Array(ev.target.result);
        let wb=window.XLSX.read(data,{type:"array"});
        let sheet=wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];
        let rows=window.XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet,{header:1,blankrows:!1});
        let headerRow=(rows[0]||[]).map(h=>String(h==null?"":h).trim()).filter(Boolean);
        setFileColumns(headerRow);
        if(headerRow.length)autoMatch(headerRow)
      }catch(err){setFileColumns([])}
    };
    reader.readAsArrayBuffer(f)
  };
  let onFileSelected=ev=>handleFileChosen(ev.target.files?.[0]);

  };

  let StepBody=({title,children})=>o.default.createElement(o.default.Fragment,null,
    o.default.createElement("div",{className:"px-6 pt-5 pb-1"},

  let picklists=store.picklists||PICKLIST_SEED;

  let visible=isRehrig?picklists:picklists.filter(f=>f.persona==="SP");
  let[search,setSearch]=(0,o.useState)(""),[objFilter,setObjFilter]=(0,o.useState)("All");
  let[selectedKey,setSelectedKey]=(0,o.useState)(null);
  let[sliderState,setSliderState]=(0,o.useState)(null);
  let[valueSearch,setValueSearch]=(0,o.useState)("");
  let[controllingFilter,setControllingFilter]=(0,o.useState)("All");
  let fkey=f=>`${f.objectApi}::${f.fieldApi}`;
  let objects=[...new Set(visible.map(f=>f.object))].sort();
  let filtered=visible.filter(f=>(objFilter==="All"||f.object===objFilter)&&(search===""||f.field.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())||f.object.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())));
  let selected=selectedKey?visible.find(f=>fkey(f)===selectedKey):null;
  let labelCounts={};
  visible.forEach(f=>{let k2=`${f.object}::${f.field}`;labelCounts[k2]=(labelCounts[k2]||0)+1});
  let isDup=f=>labelCounts[`${f.object}::${f.field}`]>1;
  let controllingField=selected&&selected.controllingField?picklists.find(f=>f.fieldApi===selected.controllingField&&f.object===selected.object):null;
  let controllingOptions=controllingField?controllingField.values.filter(v=>v.active).map(v=>v.label):[];

            )))

        )
      ),

    )
  }

  let grouped={};

  )

function PicklistValueSliderV2({mode,value,controllingFieldLabel,controllingOptions,onClose,onSave,onToggleActive}){
  let[visible,setVisible]=(0,o.useState)(!1);
  (0,o.useEffect)(()=>{let raf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>setVisible(!0));return()=>cancelAnimationFrame(raf)},[]);
  let close=()=>{setVisible(!1);setTimeout(onClose,200)};
  let[label,setLabel]=(0,o.useState)(value?.label||"");
  let[key,setKey]=(0,o.useState)(value?.api||"");
  let[isDefault,setIsDefault]=(0,o.useState)(value?.default||!1);
  let[applies,setApplies]=(0,o.useState)(()=>new Set(value?.controllingValues||[]));
  let[appliesSearch,setAppliesSearch]=(0,o.useState)("");
  let canSave=label.trim().length>0;
  let isEdit=mode==="edit";
  let active=value?.active!==!1;
  let isDependent=!!controllingFieldLabel;
  let toggleApplies=cv=>setApplies(prev=>{let n=new Set(prev);n.has(cv)?n.delete(cv):n.add(cv);return n});
  let visibleOptions=(controllingOptions||[]).filter(cv=>appliesSearch===""||cv.toLowerCase().includes(appliesSearch.toLowerCase()));
  return o.default.createElement("div",{className:"fixed inset-0 z-[70]"},
    o.default.createElement("div",{className:"absolute inset-0 transition-opacity",onClick:close,style:{background:"rgba(15,20,30,0.28)",opacity:visible?1:0,transitionDuration:"200ms"}}),
    o.default.createElement("div",{className:"absolute top-0 right-0 h-full flex flex-col shadow-2xl",onClick:ev=>ev.stopPropagation(),style:{width:isDependent?"36vw":"32vw",minWidth:isDependent?460:420,maxWidth:isDependent?600:560,background:l.surface,borderLeft:`1px solid ${l.borderHi}`,boxShadow:"-24px 0 60px -20px rgba(0,0,0,0.25)",transform:visible?"translateX(0)":"translateX(100%)",transition:"transform .22s cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1)"}},
      o.default.createElement("div",{className:"flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 shrink-0",style:{borderBottom:`1px solid ${l.border}`}},
        o.default.createElement("div",{className:"flex items-center gap-2"},
          o.default.createElement("div",{className:"w-1 h-6 rounded-full",style:{background:l.violet}}),
          o.default.createElement("div",{className:"text-[15px] font-semibold",style:{color:l.text}},isEdit?"Edit Value":"Add Value")
        ),
        o.default.createElement("button",{onClick:close,className:"w-8 h-8 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center",style:{color:l.muted}},o.default.createElement(be,{size:14}))
      ),
      o.default.createElement("div",{className:"flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto px-6 py-5 space-y-4"},
        isEdit&&!active&&o.default.createElement("div",{className:"rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-[11.5px]",style:{background:l.rose+"14",border:`1px solid ${l.rose}44`,color:l.rose}},"This value is currently inactive. Reactivate it below if it should be selectable again."),

        )

function woLatLng(wo){
  let seedStr=(wo.id||wo.workOrderNumber||"")+"";
  let seed=0;for(let i=0;i<seedStr.length;i++)seed+=seedStr.charCodeAt(i)*(i+7);
  let rand=n=>{seed=(seed*9301+49297)%233280;return(seed/233280)*n};
  let baseLat=53.5461,baseLng=-113.4938;
  return{lat:baseLat+(rand(1)-0.5)*0.1,lng:baseLng+(rand(1)-0.5)*0.16};
}

  let addCondition=()=>setConditions(c=>[...c,{field:cfg.filterFields[0]?.key||"",operator:"equals",value:""}]);

    else if(pl.dateRange==="week"){setStartDate("2026-06-24");setEndDate("2026-07-01")}

function DispatchLandingV2({account:acct,store,setStore}){
  let[filterMode,setFilterMode]=(0,o.useState)("open");
  let today=new Date();
  let toISO=d=>d.toISOString().slice(0,10);
  let[dateFrom,setDateFrom]=(0,o.useState)(toISO(new Date(today.getTime()-6*86400000)));
  let[dateTo,setDateTo]=(0,o.useState)(toISO(today));
  let[collapsed,setCollapsed]=(0,o.useState)(()=>new Set());
  let[builderState,setBuilderState]=(0,o.useState)(null);
  let[toast,setToast]=(0,o.useState)(null);
  let showToast=(title,msg)=>{setToast({title,msg});setTimeout(()=>setToast(null),2800)};
  let dispatches=(store.dispatches||[]).filter(d=>d.account===acct.name);
  let rows=filterMode==="open"?dispatches.filter(d=>d.status!=="Complete"):dispatches.filter(d=>d.status==="Complete"&&d.routeDate>=dateFrom&&d.routeDate<=dateTo);
  let groups={};
  rows.forEach(d=>{let key=(d.serviceType&&d.serviceType[0])||"Other";(groups[key]=groups[key]||[]).push(d)});
  let sectionKeys=Object.keys(groups).sort();
  let statsFor=d=>{
    let seedStr=d.id||"";let seed=0;for(let i=0;i<seedStr.length;i++)seed+=seedStr.charCodeAt(i);
    let rand=n=>{seed=(seed*9301+49297)%233280;return Math.floor((seed/233280)*n)};
    let realTotal=d.routesData?d.routesData.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.ids.length,0):0;
    let total=realTotal>0?realTotal:Math.max(1,rand(6)+1);
    let closed=Math.round(total*(d.progress||0)/100);
    let remaining=total-closed;
    let attempted=d.status==="Complete"?0:rand(remaining+1);
    let planned=remaining-attempted;
    return{total,closed,attempted,planned}
  };
  let toggleSection=k=>setCollapsed(prev=>{let n=new Set(prev);n.has(k)?n.delete(k):n.add(k);return n});
  if(builderState)return o.default.createElement(DispatchBuilderV2,{account:acct,store,setStore,editingDispatch:builderState==="new"?null:builderState,onToast:showToast,onClose:()=>setBuilderState(null)});
  return o.default.createElement("div",{className:"max-w-[1400px] mx-auto p-6 fade-in relative"},
    toast&&o.default.createElement("div",{className:"fixed top-6 right-6 z-[70] rounded-lg px-4 py-3 flex items-start gap-2.5",style:{background:"#FCF3CF",border:"1px solid #F1C40F",boxShadow:l.shadowLg,width:280}},
      o.default.createElement("span",{style:{color:l.mint,fontSize:"16px"}},"\u2713"),
      o.default.createElement("div",{className:"flex-1"},
        o.default.createElement("div",{className:"text-[12.5px] font-semibold",style:{color:"#7D6608"}},toast.title),
        o.default.createElement("div",{className:"text-[11.5px]",style:{color:"#7D6608"}},toast.msg)
      ),
      o.default.createElement("button",{onClick:()=>setToast(null),style:{color:"#7D6608"}},"\u2716")
    ),
    o.default.createElement("div",{className:"flex items-center justify-between mb-4"},
      o.default.createElement("div",{className:"flex items-center gap-3"},
        o.default.createElement("button",{onClick:()=>{},className:"h-9 px-4 rounded-lg text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-white",style:{background:l.violet}},"Refresh"),
        o.default.createElement("select",{value:filterMode,onChange:ev=>setFilterMode(ev.target.value),style:{height:36,padding:"0 10px",borderRadius:6,border:`1px solid ${l.border}`,fontSize:"12.5px",minWidth:120}},
          o.default.createElement("option",{value:"open"},"Open"),
          o.default.createElement("option",{value:"completed"},"Completed")
        ),
        filterMode==="completed"&&o.default.createElement("div",{className:"flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px]",style:{color:l.text}},
          o.default.createElement("span",{className:"font-medium"},"Display Completed Routes Between:"),
          o.default.createElement("input",{type:"date",value:dateFrom,onChange:ev=>setDateFrom(ev.target.value),style:{height:30,padding:"0 6px",borderRadius:6,border:`1px solid ${l.border}`,fontSize:"12px"}}),
          o.default.createElement("span",null,"And:"),
          o.default.createElement("input",{type:"date",value:dateTo,onChange:ev=>setDateTo(ev.target.value),style:{height:30,padding:"0 6px",borderRadius:6,border:`1px solid ${l.border}`,fontSize:"12px"}})
        )
      ),
      o.default.createElement("button",{onClick:()=>setBuilderState("new"),className:"h-9 px-4 rounded-lg text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-white",style:{background:l.violet}},"Create New Dispatch")
    ),
    sectionKeys.length===0?o.default.createElement("div",{className:"rounded-xl text-center py-12",style:{background:l.surface,border:`1px solid ${l.border}`,color:l.faint}},filterMode==="completed"?"No Dispatch Records Found within Specified Date Range":"No open dispatches."):
    sectionKeys.map(key=>o.default.createElement("div",{key,className:"mb-5 rounded-xl overflow-hidden",style:{border:`1px solid ${l.border}`,boxShadow:l.shadow}},
      o.default.createElement("div",{onClick:()=>toggleSection(key),className:"h-10 px-4 flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer",style:{background:l.violet,color:"#fff"}},
        o.default.createElement("span",{className:"text-[13px] font-semibold"},key),
        o.default.createElement("span",{style:{transform:collapsed.has(key)?"rotate(-90deg)":"none",transition:"transform 0.15s"}},"\u25be")
      ),
      !collapsed.has(key)&&o.default.createElement(o.default.Fragment,null,
        o.default.createElement("div",{className:"h-8 px-4 flex items-center text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide",style:{background:"#B9B9B9",color:"#fff"}},"Current/Prior Date"),
        o.default.createElement("div",{className:"overflow-x-auto"},
          o.default.createElement("table",{className:"w-full text-[12px]",style:{minWidth:900}},
            o.default.createElement("thead",null,o.default.createElement("tr",{style:{background:l.canvas}},
              ["Dispatch Date","Dispatch ID","Dispatch Status","Truck Name(s)","Closed","Attempted","Planned","Total Work Orders"].map(h=>o.default.createElement("th",{key:h,className:"text-left px-3 py-2 border-b hairline whitespace-nowrap"},h))
            )),
            o.default.createElement("tbody",null,groups[key].map((d,idx)=>{let st=statsFor(d);return o.default.createElement("tr",{key:d.id,className:"cursor-pointer",onClick:()=>setBuilderState(d),onMouseEnter:ev=>ev.currentTarget.style.background=l.canvas,onMouseLeave:ev=>ev.currentTarget.style.background="transparent",style:{borderTop:idx===0?"none":`1px solid ${l.border}`}},
              o.default.createElement("td",{className:"px-3 py-2 whitespace-nowrap",style:{color:l.violet}},d.routeDate),
              o.default.createElement("td",{className:"px-3 py-2 whitespace-nowrap",style:{color:l.violet,fontWeight:500}},d.dispatchNumber),
              o.default.createElement("td",{className:"px-3 py-2 whitespace-nowrap"},d.status),
              o.default.createElement("td",{className:"px-3 py-2 whitespace-nowrap"},d.truck||"\u2014"),
              o.default.createElement("td",{className:"px-3 py-2"},st.closed),
              o.default.createElement("td",{className:"px-3 py-2"},st.attempted),
              o.default.createElement("td",{className:"px-3 py-2"},st.planned),
              o.default.createElement("td",{className:"px-3 py-2"},st.total)
            )}))
          )
        )
      )
    ))
  )
}

var WO_SERVICE_TYPES=["Residential","Commercial","Industrial"];
var WO_REQUEST_TYPES=["Deliver","Remove","Missed Pickup","Inactive Account Removal","RFID Failure","Exchange","Repair"];
var WO_ASSET_TYPES=["Trash","Recycling","Organics","Yard Waste"];
var WO_ASSET_SIZES=["18 Gallon","32 Gallon","64 Gallon","96 Gallon","120 Liter","240 Liter","360 Liter","2 Cubic Yard","4 Cubic Yard","6 Cubic Yard","8 Cubic Yard"];
function diffWorkOrderChanges(before,after){
  let changes=[];
  let labelFor={caseStatus:"Status",serviceType:"Service Type",requestType:"Request Type",assetType:"Asset Type",quantitySize:"Asset Size",woNotes:"Work Order Notes",hotTicket:"Hot Ticket",dueDate:"Due Date",requestDate:"Requested Date",resolutionCode:"Resolution Code",contactName:"Contact"};
  Object.keys(labelFor).forEach(k=>{
    let a=before[k],b=after[k];
    if(String(a||"")!==String(b||"")){
      if(typeof a==="boolean"||typeof b==="boolean")changes.push(`Changed ${labelFor[k]} from ${a?"true":"false"} to ${b?"true":"false"}.`);
      else changes.push(`Changed ${labelFor[k]} from "${a||"(blank)"}" to "${b||"(blank)"}".`)
    }
  });
  return changes
}

function WoitStepBar({step,flow,onStepClick}){
  let steps=[
    {n:1,label:"Type"},
    {n:2,label:"Upload"},
    {n:3,label:"Mapping"},
    {n:4,label:"Preview"},
    {n:5,label:"Processing"},
    {n:6,label:flow==="close"?"Closed":"Imported"}
  ];
  return o.default.createElement("div",{className:"flex items-stretch px-5 pt-4 pb-3 shrink-0",style:{background:l.canvas}},
    steps.map((s,idx)=>{
      let state=s.n<step?"done":s.n===step?"active":"upcoming";
      let clickable=state==="done"&&s.n!==5&&!!onStepClick;
      let bg=state==="done"?l.mint:state==="active"?l.violet:l.surfaceHi2;
      let fg=state==="upcoming"?l.faint:"#fff";
      let clip=idx===0?"polygon(0 0,90% 0,100% 50%,90% 100%,0 100%)":idx===steps.length-1?"polygon(10% 0,100% 0,100% 100%,10% 100%,0% 50%)":"polygon(0 0,90% 0,100% 50%,90% 100%,0 100%,10% 50%)";
      return o.default.createElement("div",{key:s.n,onClick:clickable?()=>onStepClick(s.n):undefined,title:clickable?`Back to ${s.label}`:undefined,className:"flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold h-8"+(clickable?" hover:opacity-90":""),style:{flex:1,marginLeft:idx===0?0:-14,background:bg,color:fg,clipPath:clip,zIndex:steps.length-idx,transition:"background 0.25s ease",cursor:clickable?"pointer":"default"}},
        o.default.createElement("span",{style:{opacity:state==="upcoming"?0.85:1}},state==="done"?"\u2713":s.n),
        o.default.createElement("span",{className:"tracking-wide"},s.label)
      )
    })
  )
}

function DispatchBuilderV2({account:acct,store,setStore,onClose,editingDispatch,onToast}){
  let profiles=store.dispatchProfiles||DISPATCH_PROFILES_SEED;
  let trucks=store.trucks||[];
  let allWO=(store.workOrders||[]).filter(w=>w.account===acct.name);
  let matchProfileId=()=>{if(!editingDispatch)return"";let p=profiles.find(pp=>pp.name===editingDispatch.maintenanceRouteProfile);return p?p.id:""};
  let[profileId,setProfileId]=(0,o.useState)(matchProfileId);
  let[routeDate,setRouteDate]=(0,o.useState)(editingDispatch?.routeDate||"2026-09-21");
  let[status,setStatus]=(0,o.useState)(editingDispatch?.status==="Complete"?"Planned":(editingDispatch?editingDispatch.status:"Planned")==="Scheduled"?"Planned":"Planned");
  let[publishedId,setPublishedId]=(0,o.useState)(editingDispatch?editingDispatch.id:null);
  let[showProfiles,setShowProfiles]=(0,o.useState)(!1);
  let[viewMode,setViewMode]=(0,o.useState)("grid");
  let[selectedIds,setSelectedIds]=(0,o.useState)(()=>new Set());
  let[onlySelected,setOnlySelected]=(0,o.useState)(!1);
  let[routes,setRoutes]=(0,o.useState)(()=>editingDispatch?.routesData&&editingDispatch.routesData.length?editingDispatch.routesData.map(r=>({...r,ids:[...r.ids]})):[{id:"R1",label:"Route 1",ids:[],truck:""}]);
  let[activeRoute,setActiveRoute]=(0,o.useState)(0);
  let[moveTarget,setMoveTarget]=(0,o.useState)("");
  let[mapSearch,setMapSearch]=(0,o.useState)("");
  let[truckQuery,setTruckQuery]=(0,o.useState)(""),[truckDropdown,setTruckDropdown]=(0,o.useState)(!1);
  let[showInventory,setShowInventory]=(0,o.useState)(!1);
  let[optimizing,setOptimizing]=(0,o.useState)(!1);
  let[confirmDelete,setConfirmDelete]=(0,o.useState)(!1);
  let dragIdsRef=(0,o.useRef)([]);
  let[dragOverTarget,setDragOverTarget]=(0,o.useState)(null);
  let profile=profiles.find(p=>p.id===profileId);
  let assignedIds=new Set(routes.flatMap(r=>r.ids));
  let pool=profile?allWO.filter(w=>!assignedIds.has(w.id)):[];
  let visiblePool=onlySelected?pool.filter(w=>selectedIds.has(w.id)):pool;
  let toggleSelect=id=>setSelectedIds(prev=>{let n=new Set(prev);n.has(id)?n.delete(id):n.add(id);return n});
  let clusters=(0,o.useMemo)(()=>{
    let cells={};
    pool.forEach(w=>{let{lat,lng}=woLatLng(w);let key=Math.round(lat/0.02)+","+Math.round(lng/0.02);(cells[key]=cells[key]||{lat,lng,items:[]}).items.push(w)});
    return Object.values(cells)
  },[pool.map(w=>w.id).join(",")]);
  let latRange=[53.44,53.65],lngRange=[-113.62,-113.36];
  let posFor=(lat,lng)=>({x:((lng-lngRange[0])/(lngRange[1]-lngRange[0]))*100,y:100-((lat-latRange[0])/(latRange[1]-latRange[0]))*100});
  let clusterPalette=[l.violet,l.rose,l.mint,l.amber];
  let moveSelectedToRoute=()=>{
    let target=moveTarget||routes[activeRoute]?.id;
    if(!target||selectedIds.size===0)return;
    setRoutes(prev=>prev.map(r=>r.id===target?{...r,ids:[...r.ids,...selectedIds]}:r));
    setSelectedIds(new Set())
  };
  let moveIdsToRoute=(ids,targetRouteId)=>{
    if(!ids.length)return;
    setRoutes(prev=>prev.map(r=>r.id===targetRouteId?{...r,ids:[...new Set([...r.ids,...ids])]}:r));
    setSelectedIds(prev=>{let n=new Set(prev);ids.forEach(id=>n.delete(id));return n})
  };
  let addRoute=()=>{setRoutes(prev=>[...prev,{id:`R${Date.now()}`,label:`Route ${prev.length+1}`,ids:[],truck:""}]);setActiveRoute(routes.length)};
  let removeRoute=()=>{
    if(routes.length<=1)return;
    setRoutes(prev=>prev.filter((r,idx)=>idx!==activeRoute));
    setActiveRoute(prev=>Math.max(0,prev-1))
  };

    if(publishedId){

      setConfirmDelete(!1);
      onClose()
    }else{
      setRoutes([{id:"R1",label:"Route 1",ids:[],truck:""}]);setSelectedIds(new Set());setActiveRoute(0);setConfirmDelete(!1)
    }
  };
  let handleExport=()=>{
    let header=["Work Order #","Address","Request Type","Asset Size/Type","Requested Date"].join(",");
    let lines=routes.flatMap(r=>allWO.filter(w=>r.ids.includes(w.id)).map(w=>[w.workOrderNumber,`"${w.customerAccount||""}"`,w.requestTypeDisplay||w.requestType||"",w.quantitySize||"",w.requestDate||""].join(",")));
    let blob=new Blob([header+"\n"+lines.join("\n")],{type:"text/csv"}),url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),aTag=document.createElement("a");

  };
  let btnGhost={height:36,padding:"0 14px",borderRadius:6,fontSize:"12.5px",fontWeight:600,color:"#fff",background:l.violet,border:"none",cursor:"pointer"};
  let matchingTrucks=trucks.filter(t=>t.account===acct.name&&(t.truckName||"").toLowerCase().includes(truckQuery.toLowerCase()));

    o.default.createElement("div",{className:"flex items-center gap-6 mb-1"},
      o.default.createElement("span",{className:"text-[12px] font-semibold",style:{color:l.mint}},"Deliver (",data.deliverTotal,")"),
      o.default.createElement("span",{className:"text-[12px] font-semibold",style:{color:l.rose}},"Remove (",data.removeTotal,")")
    ),
    Object.keys(data.deliver).length===0&&Object.keys(data.remove).length===0?o.default.createElement("div",{className:"text-[11.5px]",style:{color:l.faint}},"No deliver/remove items."):
    o.default.createElement("ul",{className:"text-[11.5px] pl-4",style:{color:l.muted,listStyle:"disc"}},
      Object.entries({...data.deliver}).map(([k,v])=>o.default.createElement("li",{key:"d"+k},k," (",v,")")),
      Object.entries({...data.remove}).map(([k,v])=>o.default.createElement("li",{key:"r"+k},k," (",v,") \u2014 remove"))
    )
